## Proposed scoped apply plan

Go/no-go status: `NO_GO_FOR_APPLY_UNTIL_MANUAL_REVIEW_COMPLETES`.

The first scoped apply should use only the `SAFE_TO_QUARANTINE` subset and must exclude all manual-review and do-not-touch records. Because broad Redis patterns contain both safe and manual-review records, the apply filter must include key-pattern filters, reason filters, active-state exclusion, recent-record exclusion, and active-stale exclusion.

### Allowed key patterns

- `v2:prediction:*`, excluding `v2:prediction:rl_core:*`
- `v2:signals:paper:*`
- `v2:signals:paper:*:tf`

### Allowed reason codes

- `STALE_PREDICTION_UNREPLAYABLE`
- `STALE_PAPER_SIGNAL_UNREPLAYABLE`

### Required allowed-record predicates

- `active=false`
- `trust_schema_version` is missing or absent, not `pipeline_trust_v3`
- age bucket is not `0-1h`
- no approval/routing flags such as `approved`, `pre_trade_allowed`, `routed_to_paper`, or `paper_fill_allowed`
- reason set does not include `ACTIVE_STALE_TRUST_CONTRACT_MISSING`

### Excluded key patterns

- `v2:prediction:rl_core:*`
- `v2:risk:*`
- `v2:orchestrator:*`
- `v2:paper:intents`
- `v2:live_order_transport:*`
- `v2:live_canary:*`
- `v2:market:ohlcv_closed:*`
- `v2:market:mtf_snapshot:*`
- `v2:decision:mtf_snapshot:*`
- `v2:mtf_snapshot:*`
- `v2:replay:snapshots:*`
- `v2:config*`

### Excluded reason codes

- `ACTIVE_STALE_TRUST_CONTRACT_MISSING`

Records with `STALE_PREDICTION_UNREPLAYABLE` or `STALE_PAPER_SIGNAL_UNREPLAYABLE` are still excluded if they are recent, active, approval/routing-enabled, `pipeline_trust_v3`, or under an excluded key pattern.

### Max target count expected

- `--max-targets 1536`

### Backup path

- `pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/backup.jsonl`

### Rollback instructions

- Use the JSONL backup before any apply run.
- For every backup row, restore the original `value` to the same `redis_key` with the same Redis type.
- Do not restore into live-order or exchange-order keys without separate operator approval.
- After rollback, run `export_pipeline_trust_evidence` and `verify_pipeline_trust --strict-unknown` again.

### Post-apply verification commands

```bash
./export_pipeline_trust_evidence \
  --redis-url redis://127.0.0.1:6379/0 \
  --output-dir pipeline_trust_evidence

./verify_pipeline_trust \
  --input pipeline_trust_evidence/<new_run> \
  --output-dir pipeline_trust_evidence/<new_run>/report \
  --strict-unknown
```

## Independent jq inspection commands

```bash
jq '{keys_scanned, records_targeted, mode, dry_run, backup_path, safety}' pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/quarantine_report.json

jq -r '.plan[] | [.redis_key, (.reasons|join(",")), .redis_type] | @tsv' pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/quarantine_report.json | head -50

jq '[.plan[].reasons[]] | group_by(.) | map({reason: .[0], count: length}) | sort_by(.reason)' pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/quarantine_report.json

jq -r '.plan[].redis_key' pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/quarantine_report.json | sed 's/:.*//g' | sort | uniq -c

jq -r '.plan[] | select((.redis_key|test("live_order|exchange_order|order_transport"; "i")))' pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/quarantine_report.json

jq -r 'select(.value.trust_schema_version == "pipeline_trust_v3") | .redis_key' pipeline_trust_quarantine/20260612_180857/backup.jsonl

```

## Final recommendation

`NO_GO` for blind `--apply`.

Reason: the dry-run targeted a large number of records, including records with active approval/routing flags and records that require manual review. A scoped apply should only proceed after adding CLI filters such as `--only-reason`, `--only-key-pattern`, `--exclude-key-pattern`, `--max-targets`, `--require-review-file`, and `--backup-required`, then confirming the scoped dry-run target count equals the `SAFE_TO_QUARANTINE` count in this review.
